# The Quran — Võro Draft

**DRAFT — NOT PUBLISHED**

A first-pass, engine-rendered draft translation of the Locked
English Master into Võro (võro kiilʼ, ISO 639-3: vro), in the
modern Võro literary standard (Võro Institute norm) only. Seto,
Mulgi and Tartu are expressly out of scope. Prepared under the
ruling of Robert Tilford, 2026-11-27, as a gift draft for the
Võro-speaking community: attribution requested, translator rights
retained, no claim of community invitation or endorsement.

**Base and sources.** Every seated Võro form is attested in open
sources held at the desk: the GiellaLT lang-vro morphological
resources (about 20,632 stems; LGPL v3) and its corpus word-form
list (81,133 forms), and English Wiktionary Võro entries via
kaikki (CC BY-SA; 606 headwords, 7,543 forms). The Võro Institute
dictionary (synaq.org; Iva, *Võro-eesti synaraamat*, 2002) and the
historic South Estonian *Wastne Testament* (1686, public domain)
were used as controls by fact and citation only — their wording
was never reproduced, and no 1686 form was seated as modern Võro.
No modern Võro Bible was found (searched 27 November 2026); that
absence is part of this draft's record. No Standard Estonian form
was seated unless independently attested for Võro, and no Seto
form was seated.

**Honest status.** This draft has NOT been independently checked
and has NOT been published. It awaits a fresh independent checker
who did not build it. Of 6,236 verses, 6009 carry
one or more visible GAP markers (29698 markers,
5300 distinct English terms) where no
attested Võro form was held: the gap was held open rather than
filled with an invented or borrowed word. The verified dictionary
behind the seated text holds 504 English–Võro entries; 27 further
concepts (among them camel, mosque, prayer as a verb, forgive,
resurrection, worship) had no verified seat and stand as GAPs
throughout.

**Plain-style devices, disclosed.** Verbs seat in their dictionary
(ma-infinitive) form, with person and tense carried by subject and
time words; nouns seat in the nominative, with the regular plural
suffix -q where the Master has a plural; articles and the genitive
linker "of" seat as zero, as Võro has no articles; the case
markers lõ (to) and h (in/into) seat as separate words; the
vocative particle "O" seats as zero. Personal names and scripture
titles are carried in the Master's forms.

**The Basmalah.** "Jumala nimeh, Arm, Arm." Both mercy names rest
on the one attested mercy stem arm; that single-stem seating is
disclosed here rather than hidden. Surah 1:1 is the Basmalah;
every other surah except 9 carries it as an unnumbered opening
line (112 unnumbered lines).

**Novelty wording.** No "first ever" claim is made. The bounded
finding of the scouting brief stands as: the first complete Võro
Qur'an translation found — after research (27 November 2026);
not found is not proven non-existent.

---
## 1. The Opening

1. Jumala nimeh, Arm, Arm.
2. kiitmä olõma lõ Jumal, Jumal ilmq.
3. Arm, Arm.
4. ⟦GAP: master⟧ päiv kohus.
5. tuu om saq miq ⟦GAP: worship⟧, ja pääl saq miq kutsma jaos avitama.
6. juhatama meid lõ õige tii.
7. tii nuuq saq om õnnistama, ei nuuq vasta kedä sääl om ⟦GAP: anger⟧, ei nuuq kiä ommaq ⟦GAP: misguided⟧.
